Revision 88 / Serial number 840726

West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 100
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Behind House: open window, then 'west' into the Kitchen. The +10 was recorded on that west move at step 4.
2. Living Room: take lantern, push rug, open trap door, then 'down'. The +25 was recorded on 'down'; it is a one-time move.
3. After arriving in a pitch-black room, spend the first turn lighting the lantern and reading the new room text before any exit.
4. Gallery 'take art' recorded +4. In new lit rooms, take portable items and compare the score before and after.
5. Judge goals by recorded score events. Ignore any 'up' goal: it recorded 0 at Kitchen, and the +25 came from 'down'.
6. In each new lit room, try every unvisited exit once, then mark the room finished and move to a new frontier.
7. Run 'inventory' after 'turn on lantern' and before leaving each dark arrival room, so the light source is verified.
8. After 10 moves with no score or new room, leave the loop and try an exit or room not yet visited.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Never take exits while the observation says pitch black; Cellar east and West from East Chasm both ended in grue deaths in earlier runs.
2. Don't enter or linger in the Troll Room. It cost -10 and ended this episode, and earlier it killed run 2.
3. Don't shuttle Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio. About 60 steps there scored nothing after the +4.
4. Don't drop and retake treasure, or take the manual, hoping for score. Those actions recorded 0.
5. Don't chase 'return to Living Room' after 'down'. The trap door is barred, and repeated tries wasted dozens of steps.
6. Don't open with North House or other detours. Steps 1-2 delayed the route that scored.
7. Don't give items to the troll or interact with it. It recorded 0 and the Troll Room ended the run.
8. Don't write state notes that contradict the action, such as 'turning on lantern' beside 'take sword'. Confirm the lantern is lit through inventory.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Historian episode review — episode 9
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 8 positive + 8 negative (configured per category: 8)

The Actor's recorded scoring was solid but uneven. Behind House west gave +10, Living Room down +25, Gallery take art +4, putting the painting in the case +6, taking the egg +5, and depositing treasures +5. The visible trajectory is truncated, so I can't credit specific reasoning for those. The visible tail is clearly ineffective: dozens of Cellar/East Chasm/Gallery/Studio shuttles scored 0. The symbolic layer kept re-issuing the stale 'up' goal. The Actor obeyed it instead of overriding it with a never-tried exit or new room. Its own side actions (drop lunch, take pepper, open/close sack) also scored 0. The final move north from East Chasm happened in the dark, and a grue killed the run for -10. Its note about lighting the lantern did not match the move. Next episode: ignore 'up' and return goals, verify light first, and leave loops after about 10 moves.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Behind House: open window, then 'west' into the Kitchen. The +10 was recorded on that west move.
2. Living Room 'down' recorded +25. Trust recorded events over any stored 'up' goal, which is stale.
3. Gallery 'take art' recorded +4. In new lit rooms, take portable items and compare the score before and after.
4. Putting the painting in the Living Room trophy case recorded +6. Deposit carried treasures there, then verify the score.
5. Up a Tree 'take egg' recorded +5. Explore outdoor rooms beyond the house for new items and rooms.
6. In any dark arrival room, light the lantern and confirm with inventory before choosing an exit.
7. In each lit room, try every unvisited exit once, mark the room finished, then head to a new room.
8. After 10 moves with no score or new room, abandon the loop and pick an untried exit.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Never move while the room is dark. The final move north from East Chasm ended in grue death (-10).
2. Ignore stale 'up' and return-home goals. The trap door is barred after 'down', and repeating them wasted over 200 steps.
3. Don't shuttle Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio. The repeated cycles recorded 0 each time.
4. Avoid the Troll Room: it cost -10 earlier and killed run 2. Don't give it items or fight it.
5. Don't give items to the troll. Giving the sword recorded 0 and preceded death.
6. Don't drop and retake lunch or pepper hoping for score. Those actions recorded 0.
7. Don't open and close the sack repeatedly. Four toggles in the Cellar scored nothing.
8. Don't write state notes that contradict the action, like 'light lantern' beside a move into Forest. Verify with inventory.
